### Slide 28

**Text**

Record desk adds drove dip in occupancy in Q3'19 / Building Occupancy(1) / Total Memberships / Mature(2) / Non-Mature / Total / 89% / 89% / 88% / 89% / 91% / 91% / 90% / 88% / 88% / 89% / 87% / 73% / 78% / 81% / 82% / 83% / 85% / 85% / 80% / 81% / 83% / 80% / 69% / 75% / 78% / 80% / 80% / 82% / 83% / 78% / 78% / 80% / 76% / 2017 Avg. / Occ: 78% / 2018 Avg. / Occ: 84% / YTD 2019 / Avg. Occ: / 81% / Desks / Added △ / 1Q '17 / 2Q '17 / 3Q '17 / 4Q '17 / 1Q '18 / 2Q '18 / 3Q '18 / 4Q '18 / 1Q '19 / 2Q '19 / 3Q '19 / 22K / 25K / 22K / 32K / 34K / 48K / 49K / 105K / 70K / 55K / 108K / Note: Figures exclude IndiaCo locations. Components may not add due to rounding. / (1) Occupancy reflects total physical memberships divided by total desks. / (2) Represents occupancy at locations that have been open for greater than 24 months / WeWork Memberships / On Demand / +88% YoY / 580K / 503K / 446K / 387K / 310K / 42K / 31K / 29K / 30K / 22K / 539K / 472K / 416K / 357K / 287K / 3Q '18 / 4Q '18 / 1Q '19 / 2Q '19 / 3Q '19 / Occupancy(1) / 85% / 80% / 81% / 83% / 80% / Mature Location / Occupancy(2) / 90% / 88% / 88% / 89% / 87% / 28

**Layout**

Two equal columns beneath a full-width title: left column a line chart under the header 'Building Occupancy(1)' with a 1Q '17 to 3Q '19 desk-added table beneath it, right column a stacked bar chart under 'Total Memberships' with an occupancy table beneath it. Title is left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 3% from left), each column header is underlined by a thin dark rule, footnotes sit bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: line chart titled Building Occupancy(1), y-axis unlabelled percent (about 60% to 100% implied, no axis drawn), x-axis quarters 1Q '17 to 3Q '19 with no axis line. Three series with printed labels: Mature(2) teal line 89%, 89%, 88%, 89%, 91%, 91%, 90%, 88%, 88%, 89%, 87%; Non-Mature yellow line 69%, 75%, 78%, 80%, 80%, 82%, 83%, 78%, 78%, 80%, 76% (the 83% and 80% values near the yellow line are estimated from label placement); Total dark slate line 73%, 78%, 81%, 82%, 83%, 85%, 85%, 80%, 81%, 83%, 80%. Legend above the plot: Mature(2) teal, Non-Mature yellow, Total dark slate. Three grey dashed boxes annotate averages: 2017 Avg. Occ: 78% under 1Q-4Q '17, 2018 Avg. Occ: 84% under 1Q-4Q '18, YTD 2019 Avg. Occ: 81% under 2019. A red dashed ellipse highlights the 3Q '19 points (87%, 80%, 76%). Below the chart a grey-shaded table with rows Desks Added with 1Q '17 22K, 2Q '17 25K, 3Q '17 22K, 4Q '17 32K, 1Q '18 34K, 2Q '18 48K, 3Q '18 49K, 4Q '18 105K, 1Q '19 70K, 2Q '19 55K, 3Q '19 108K. Right: stacked column chart titled Total Memberships, no axis, columns with baseline rule, bottom segment WeWork Memberships in teal and top segment On Demand in light teal; values 3Q '18 287K + 22K = 310K, 4Q '18 357K + 30K = 387K, 1Q '19 416K + 29K = 446K, 2Q '19 472K + 31K = 503K, 3Q '19 539K + 42K = 580K. Legend top right with teal and light teal squares. A thin dark arrow rises from lower left to upper right above the columns, labelled +88% YoY in bold italic. **Layout**

Full-width title sits top-left across the slide at about 3% from the left edge and 4.5% from the top, with a two-column body below: a left chart column (about 3% to 48% wide) and a right table column (about 52% to 97% wide), each headed by a sub-heading with a black rule beneath. Text is left-aligned throughout; the footnote runs along the bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right; margins are about 3% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled Mature Building Occupancy with superscript (1). Categories on the x-axis: 1Q ’18, 2Q ’18, 3Q ’18, 4Q ’18, 1Q ’19, 2Q ’19, 3Q ’19. No y-axis is drawn; values are printed as data labels (percent of desks, mature buildings). Series and printed values: Europe, Middle East & Africa (light steel blue, labelled EMEA): 93%, 94%, 94%, 92%, 92%, 95%, 89%. US, Canada & Israel (dark navy, labelled USCI): 91%, 91%, 89%, 88%, 89%, 90%, 89%. Latin America (red, labelled LatAm): no value at 1Q ’18 or 2Q ’18; 3Q ’18 87%, 4Q ’18 91%, 1Q ’19 89%, 2Q ’19 81%, 3Q ’19 88%. Pacific (yellow, labelled Pacific): no value at 1Q ’18; 2Q ’18 89%, 3Q ’18 77%, 4Q ’18 81%, 1Q ’19 77%, 2Q ’19 77%, 3Q ’19 69%. China (teal, labelled China): 2Q ’18 87%, 3Q ’18 83%, 4Q ’18 77%, 1Q ’19 75%, 2Q ’19 79%, 3Q ’19 76%. India is listed in the legend in a similar teal; no separate India series with values is distinguishable. The 3Q ’19 points for China (76%), Pacific (69%) and EMEA (89%) are highlighted by a red dashed ellipse. Legend below the x-axis in two rows, each with a short coloured line swatch: US, Canada & Israel (navy); Europe, Middle East & Africa (steel blue); China (teal); India (teal); Latin America (red); Pacific (yellow). Right side is a table-like list with a header Key Drivers and five rows, each with a coloured bold italic label and a black square bullet: rows as listed in text. Lines are plain flat strokes with no photographs or logos on the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate style. Title in a dark charcoal #222222 geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like), regular weight. Sub-headings in charcoal #222222, semibold. Body text in charcoal #222222 regular. Accent colours: EMEA steel blue #6E9BB5, USCI navy #2E3E4E, LatAm red #B03A2E, Pacific yellow #F5B800, China teal #3A8FA0, highlight ellipse red #D62B2B dashed. Title about 6 percent of slide height; sub-headings about 3.7 percent; body text about 2.1 percent; footnote about 0.8 percent. Rules are solid black 1 px lines under sub-headings and the Key Drivers header; row separators are thin grey #BFBFBF lines. No shapes have fills, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Text**

Non-GAAP reconciliation: H1 '19 location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue and Location contribution margin for buildings at various maturity cohorts
($ in millions) / 0-6 Months / 7-12 Months / 13 – 24 Months / +25 Months / Other(2) / Total Consolidated – All locations
Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue calculated in accordance with GAAP:
Membership and service revenue / $163 / $272 / $345 / $547 / $22 / $1,349
Cost of membership and service revenue:
Location operating expenses (exclusive of depreciation and amortization shown separately below) / (201) / (258) / (288) / (435) / (51) / (1,233)
Depreciation and amortization - relating to location operating expenses / (38) / (47) / (52) / (85) / (9) / (230)
Total cost of membership and service revenue / (239) / (305) / (340) / (520) / (60) / (1,463)
Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue / ($76) / ($33) / $5 / $28 / ($38) / ($114)

Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue / ($76) / ($33) / $5 / $28 / ($38) / ($114)

Depreciation and amortization – relating to location operating expenses / 38 / 47 / 52 / 85 / 9 / 230

Location contribution margin / ($38) / $14 / $57 / $102 / (4) / 142

(2) The “Other” cohort relates to membership and service revenue related to management fees earned for providing services to unconsolidated locations in India and revenue, location operating expenses, and depreciation relating to locations that also serve as headquarters locations that are not included in the Company’s cohort analysis, as performance of these locations is not representative of our open locations

**Layout**

Single full-width table slide with a three-line title across the top, left-aligned at about 4% margin, and a footnote block and slide number along the bottom. The table spans the full content width with a label column on the left and six numeric columns on the right, separated by horizontal rules under the header and above the subtotal rows.

**Visuals**

A financial reconciliation table with no chart or image. Columns: row label (left, unnamed), 0-6 Months, 7-12 Months, 13 – 24 Months, +25 Months, Other(2), Total Consolidated – All locations (right-aligned numeric values, negatives in parentheses). Rows: Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue calculated in accordance with GAAP (section header); Membership and service revenue $163 / $272 / $345 / $547 / $22 / $1,349; Cost of membership and service revenue (sub-header); Location operating expenses (exclusive of depreciation and amortization shown separately below) (201) / (258) / (288) / (435) / (51) / (1,233); Depreciation and amortization - relating to location operating expenses (38) / (47) / (52) / (85) / (9) / (230); Total cost of membership and service revenue (239) / (305) / (340) / (520) / (60) / (1,463); Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue ($76) / ($33) / $5 / $28 / ($38) / ($114) bold with rule above; Location gross profit (loss) margin percentage (47%) / (12%) / 1% / 5% / blank / (8%); section header Reconciliation of GAAP location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue to non-GAAP location contribution margin:; Location gross profit (loss) from membership and service revenue ($76) / ($33) / $5 / $28 / ($38) / ($114); Add: (sub-header); Depreciation and amortization – relating to location operating expenses 38 / 47 / 52 / 85 / 9 / 230; Stock-based compensation expense (as included in location operating expenses)(1) – / – / – / – / 26 / 26; Location contribution margin ($38) / $14 / $57 / $102 / (4) / 142 bold; Impact of non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost (as included in location operating expenses) on location contribution margin 71 / 57 / 43 / 17 / 5 / 198 bold; Location contribution margin percentage (24%) / 5% / 17% / 21% / blank / 10%; Non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost as a % of membership and service revenue 44% / 21% / 12% / 4% / blank / 15%; Benefit of non-cash amortization of lease incentives as a % of membership and service revenue (8%) / (4%) / (5%) / (5%) / blank / (5%). No chart, logos, icons or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white corporate financial slide. Title in near-black #222222 in a geometric sans-serif regular weight (similar to Gilroy or Avenir), about 5% of slide height per line with tight leading. Table text in near-black #222222, a medium-weight geometric sans-serif (similar to Gilroy Medium), about 1.8% of slide height; row labels in regular weight, subtotal rows in bold. **Layout**

Single-slide layout on a white field with the title top-left at about 4% left margin, a grey italic subtitle directly beneath it, then a full-width wide table at top with a short second table lower-left and a teal call-out box lower-right, with footnotes across the bottom and the slide number bottom-right. A dashed vertical divider separates the metric columns from the two right-hand lease columns.

**Visuals**

Two data tables and one teal call-out box; no chart, photo, or logo. Table 1 (top): columns are 1H 2019 (row labels), 1H'19 Average Desks, 1H'19 Average total memberships, Implied occupancy, Implied Enterprise membership %, Membership & service Revenue, Location Contribution Margin, Location Contribution Margin (%), Non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost as a % of membership and service revenue, and Benefit of non-cash amortization of lease incentives as a % of membership and service revenue. Rows: New York 62.0K, 57.4K, 93%, 23%, $235, $36, 15%, 13%, (5%); London 42.2K, 39.1K, 93%, 26%, $172, $28, 16%, 12%, (4%); San Francisco 21.7K, 19.6K, 90%, 57%, $87, $27, 31%, 10%, (4%); Los Angeles 21.2K, 17.4K, 82%, 22%, $59, $11, 18%, 20%, (8%); Boston 11.7K, 10.9K, 93%, 51%, $36, $9, 25%, 24%, (7%); Chicago 10.8K, 8.7K, 81%, 35%, $26, $9, 34%, 11%, (8%); Washington DC 11.2K, 9.8K, 87%, 20%, $32, $3, 9%, 18%, (7%); Top 7 markets 181K, 163K, 90%, 30%, $646, $122, 19%, 14%, (5%). Table 2 (lower left): columns As of June 1, 2019 (row labels), Desks, Total memberships, Occupancy, % Enterprise memberships. Rows: New York 63.2K, 58.6K, 93%, 23%; London 42.7K, 40.3K, 94%, 26%; San Francisco 21.7K, 20.4K, 94%, 57%; Los Angeles 21.1K, 18.3K, 87%, 22%; Boston 12.8K, 11.9K, 93%, 51%; Chicago 10.7K, 9.4K, 88%, 35%; Washington DC 11.3K, 10.1K, 89%, 20%; Top 7 markets 184K, 169K, 92%, 30%. Horizontal rules sit under the header row and above the Top 7 markets total row. A dashed blue-grey vertical line runs from just under the subtitle down past the first table's total row. **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, dark charcoal headings and body text, teal accent #408F9E for the call-out box, light grey #8A8F98 for footnotes. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham or Avenir, regular weight for title and table body with bold table headers, italic for subtitle and call-out sub-lines. Title is roughly 7% of slide height, subtitle about 2.4%, table body about 1.6%, footnotes about 1.0%. Flat shapes with no corner radius, no shadow, no gradient.

37

**Layout**

Two-part header at top-left with the title and subtitle left-aligned at about a 4% left margin, followed by a single bar chart occupying the full content width with a horizontal baseline. Below the baseline, a category label row, a bordered two-row data table spanning the same width, a small footnote at bottom-left and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart, untitled apart from the slide title, with the y-axis unit label '% of desks' at top-left in italics; no visible axis ticks or numeric scale. Four dark slate bars, each with its value printed above it: '<=0%' bar labelled 11%, '>0% to 20%' bar labelled 29%, '>20% - 40%' bar labelled 51% (tallest), '>40%' bar labelled 9%. Bar heights are proportional to the printed values (about 11%, 29%, 51%, 9%). Category x-axis labels are printed beneath the baseline, with the row heading 'Location contribution margin' at left. No legend and no highlighted points beyond the tallest bar. Below the chart a bordered table with two rows and four data columns aligned under the bars: row '# of desks' values 13K, 34K, 59K, 10K; row '% with management agreements' values 34%, 14%, 1%, --. Bars are flat, no gradient, no shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with near-black titles and body text (#231F20), bars in slate #304957, table border mid-grey #A6A6A6, axis baseline light grey #D0D0D0. Geometric humanist sans-serif typeface similar to Gotham or Avenir, title regular weight at about 7% of slide height, subtitle about 4% of slide height, body and labels about 2.5% of slide height, footnote about 1.1% of slide height, page number grey #8C8C8C about 2.6% of slide height. Thin hairline table border and baseline, square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 38

**Text**

Overall churn has declined over time driven by greater focus on / member experience and shift to Enterprise / Churn by Enterprise vs. Non-Enterprise / Average Monthly Gross Churn(1) / 2017 / 2018 / YTD 2019 / Enterprise / 3.9% / 2.9% / 2.7% / Non- Enterprise / 5.6% / 5.1% / 4.3% / Overall / 5.2% / 4.3% / 3.6% / Average Monthly Net Churn(1) / 2017 / 2018 / YTD 2019 / Enterprise / (5.0%) / (5.3%) / (5.3%) / Non- Enterprise / 1.8% / 1.6% / 1.5% / Overall / 0.4% / (0.4%) / (1.0%) / Note: Figures are as of September 1, 2019 and exclude Hot Desks and IndiaCo. Gross churn represents the total number desks moving out and downgrading at the organization level, excluding Hot Desks, divided by the beginning of period desks. Net churn represents gross churn less / upgrades from existing member organizations, excluding Hot Desks. Upgrades represent the total number of desks existing member organizations add in a given period divided by the beginning of period desks. / (1) Average represents monthly average over the last twelve months for 2017 and 2018, respectively, and last 9 months for YTD 2019. / 38

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white field with a left margin of about 4% and right margin about 4%. The two-line sentence title sits top-left at the top of the slide, followed by a subtitle and a thin horizontal rule. Two stacked tables occupy the middle, each with a centered table caption, a rule, a year header row, and three data rows; the label column is left-aligned and the three numeric columns are centered. A small footnote block sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two stacked data tables with no logos, icons or photographs. Table 1 titled 'Average Monthly Gross Churn(1)' has columns for row label, 2017, 2018 and YTD 2019, with rows Enterprise (3.9%, 2.9%, 2.7%), Non- Enterprise (5.6%, 5.1%, 4.3%) and Overall (5.2%, 4.3%, 3.6%). Table 2 titled 'Average Monthly Net Churn(1)' has the same columns, with rows Enterprise ((5.0%), (5.3%), (5.3%)), Non- Enterprise (1.8%, 1.6%, 1.5%) and Overall (0.4%, (0.4%), (1.0%)). The Enterprise row in each table is highlighted with a light blue-grey band and a dashed outline; the other rows are unhighlighted with thin grey rules between them.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with dark slate text. Title is a medium-weight geometric sans-serif (closest to Gotham or Avenir) at about 6.5% of slide height, colour #2B2D3A. Subtitle is regular-weight same family at about 3.3% of slide height, colour #2B2D3A. Table captions and headers are semi-bold at about 2.3%; body cells regular at about 2.3%; footnote about 1.1%. Accent is the highlight band #CFDDE4 with dashed outline #3C4A55. Rules are thin 1px dark slate #3C4A55 under headers and light grey #A0A8AE between rows. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Single slide with the title top-left at about 3% left margin and 6% from the top, a centred-right table block that spans about 18% to 82% of the width, a footnote block along the bottom-left from about 1% to 60% width, and the slide number at bottom-right. There is no footer band and no logo.

**Visuals**

One table, not a chart. Columns: row label column (no header), Pre-open(1), Open non-mature(2), Open mature(3), Total. A navy spanning header bar reads 'As of 3Q'19' above the column headers. Rows: 'Desks' (bold, underlined section label, no values); 'ParentCo' 450K | 338K | 182K | 969K (Total bold); '% of total' 46% | 35% | 19% | blank; 'JVs' 130K | 135K | 23K | 288K (Total bold); '% of total' 45% | 47% | 8% | blank; a black rule; 'Total WeWork Desks' 580K | 473K | 204K | 1,257K (bold); '% of total' in italics 46% | 38% | 16% | blank. Superscripts (1), (2), (3) follow the column headers. Two black rules: one under the column headers spanning the value columns, one above the Total WeWork Desks row spanning the full table width. No photographs, illustrations, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, flat, no gradients, shadows or textures. Title and footnotes in dark charcoal; table text and rules pure black; the only colour accent is the navy header bar. Title in a geometric humanist sans-serif (Gill Sans or Avenir-like), regular weight, about 5.8% of slide height. Table text in Arial/Helvetica regular with bold for totals, about 3.4% of slide height. Footnotes in a light italic sans, about 1.6% of slide height. Header bar is a square-cornered rectangle with no border.

**Review notes**

**Text**

**Layout**

Single full-width data table on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 7 to 19 percent of slide height in two lines, left-aligned to a margin of about 4 percent. The table spans from about 3 to 97 percent horizontally, with column headers at about 20 to 24 percent height and row labels left-aligned in the first column while figures are centred in the numeric columns. Footnotes sit at the bottom left above a small page number at bottom right, about 94 to 97 percent height.

### Slide 44

**Text**

wework
KPIs
October 12, 2019

**Layout**

Centered single-column stack on a plain dark field. The wework logo sits in the upper-middle, the KPIs title is centred below it, and the date line sits beneath the title. There is no title placeholder, footer, slide number or margin frame; the composition is centred on both axes.

**Visuals**

Logo: white lowercase wordmark 'wework' in a heavy high-contrast serif, spanning about 36.2% to 63.8% of slide width and 38.7% to 48.8% of slide height, rendered as a 1000 by 205 pixel image. Two small text-based images appear beneath it at the title and date positions, rendered as image-type elements, with no further graphics. A 2 by 2 pixel image sits near the bottom-right corner with no visible content. No photographs, charts, icons or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #1E1E1E covering the full slide. Flat near-black background #1E1E1E with no gradient, texture or pattern. All text and the logo are white #FFFFFF. The KPIs title is bold geometric sans-serif (close to Gilroy Bold), about 7.5% of slide height. The date line is regular-weight geometric sans-serif (close to Gilroy Regular), about 4.8% of slide height, with normal capitalisation. No accent colours, shapes, lines, shadows or corner radii.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Total Memberships Bridge / 609K / 29K / 42K / 527K / 25K / 31K / 466K / 20K / 29K / 401K / 14K / 30K / 320K / 10K / 22K / 287K / 357K / 416K / 472K / 539K / 3Q '18 / 4Q '18 / 1Q '19 / 2Q '19 / 3Q '19 / 580k Total / Memberships / (ex. India) / WeWork Memberships / On Demand / India Memberships / Note: Components may not add due to rounding. / 45

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a white field. The title sits top-left at about 4.8% from the top and 3.2% from the left edge. The stacked column chart spans the width between about 4.6% and 88% with its baseline near 81% of the height. The category labels sit under the baseline, the legend is centred in a row near 88% of the height, and a bracket note sits at the right. The footnote is bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, five columns, no chart title. Each column is stacked in three series from bottom to top: WeWork Memberships (teal #408F9E, value printed inside in white), On Demand (light blue #ADD6DE, value printed inside in dark text), India Memberships (dark slate #304957, value printed inside in white for 25K and 29K, and printed outside to the right for 10K, 14K and 20K). Column totals are printed above each column in dark text. Columns in order: 3Q '18 with WeWork 287K, On Demand 22K, India 10K, total 320K; 4Q '18 with WeWork 357K, On Demand 30K, India 14K, total 401K; 1Q '19 with WeWork 416K, On Demand 29K, India 20K, total 466K; 2Q '19 with WeWork 472K, On Demand 31K, India 25K, total 527K; 3Q '19 with WeWork 539K, On Demand 42K, India 29K, total 609K. No value axis or gridlines are shown. Category axis labels are 3Q '18, 4Q '18, 1Q '19, 2Q '19, 3Q '19. No highlighted points. Legend below the chart with three square swatches: teal WeWork Memberships, light blue On Demand, dark slate India Memberships. A grey bracket on the right spans the 3Q '19 column from its base to its top and is labelled 580k Total Memberships (ex. India). No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in near-black #222222, sans-serif geometric typeface similar to Gotham, regular weight, about 5.8% of slide height. Data labels and category labels in #222222 and white, sans-serif geometric regular weight, about 3.2% of slide height. Legend text #222222 about 3.2%. Footnote in grey #6B6B6B about 0.9%. Page number in light grey #9A9A9A about 2.8%. Column fills are flat with thin white separators between segments. Baseline is a thin light grey #DDDDDD hairline. Bracket line in grey #8C8C8C, about 0.15% stroke. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 48

**Text**

Corporate Structure / Represents Restricted Group (excl. Foreign JV's) / Lease Obligations / Parent Lease Obligations Reduce Over Time(3) / Breakdown of Parent Lease / Obligations: / $145mm Surety Bonds / $60.7mm Cash Security / $932mm Letters of Credit / $1.2b L&T Guaranty(2) / $4.2b Parent Guaranty (WeWork / Companies LLC guaranteed $4.1b) / $41.8b / $6.5b / Total Rent Commitment(1) / Total Parent Lease / Obligations / Most of our locations are leased by a 100%-owned SPE that acts as the lessee and operator of that location. / The landlord typically requires some credit support of the SPE in the form of a WeWork parent guaranty and either (1) letters of credit, (2) surety bonds, or (3) cash security based on deal- and jurisdiction-specific factors and generally tapers down as the property matures. / ($mm) / $932mm Letters of Credit / $4.2bn Parent Guaranty / 6,000 / 5,000 / 4,000 / 3,000 / 2,000 / 1,000 / - / ~52% / decrease / $520mm Letters of Credit / $1.9bn Parent Guaranty / 6/30/2019 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 / 2027 / 2028 / 2029 / 2030 / 2031 / 2032 / For a majority of Parent Guaranties, the guaranteed amount burns off over time (though usually not to zero). / The increase in Parent Guaranties in the second half of 2019 and 2020 compared to 6/30/2019 is driven by signing additional Gate A deals. Typically, a Parent Guaranty is required on day 1 of a lease. / Landlords typically require 12-18 months of rent in the form of Letters of Credits at lease commencement. Over time, Letters of Credits gradually burn off to 6-12 months of rent. / The increase in Letters of Credit in the second half of 2019 and 2020 compared to 6/30/2019 is driven by leases that we signed in 2019 but have not yet taken possession of the property or represents the additional floors that we have signed in our existing locations, but have not yet taken possession. In this scenario, we often push not to deliver Letters of Credit at first, but the amount steps up over time as we take possession of the building or additional floors. / Note: All numbers are as of June 30, 2019, per the Company's Lease Security Report. Parent guaranties and Letters of Credit that are associated with leases that commence in 2020 and beyond have been grouped within the 2019 vintage for illustrative purposes as we are aware of the additional obligations with each property and the majority of which are associated with existing properties where we plan to take on an additional floor and will realize a subsequent increase in parent lease obligations in the coming years. Since providing this information on October 20, 2019, the Company has revised these figures. / (1) Total Rent Commitment represents the future undiscounted fixed minimum least cost payments under operating and finance leases signed as of June 30, 2019. / (2) Guaranty for L&T is for the life of the lease for all contractual lease payments. / (3) Based on all leases commenced as of June 30, 2019 and excludes the L&T Guaranty. / 48

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: left column (Lease Obligations) spans roughly x 3 to 41 percent, right column (Parent Lease Obligations Reduce Over Time) spans roughly x 44 to 97 percent. The title sits top-left at about y 2 to 8 percent with an italic subtitle beneath it; each column has a section heading with a thin rule under it at about y 14 percent, bullets sit below the charts, the footnote block is bottom-left, and the page number is bottom-right at about x 97 percent, y 96 percent.

**Visuals**

Left: a two-bar column chart titled by the heading 'Lease Obligations'. Bar 1 is a teal bar labelled '$41.8b' above it, labelled on the axis 'Total Rent Commitment (1)'; it is tall, about 24 percent of slide height. Bar 2 is a short teal bar labelled '$6.5b' above it, labelled 'Total Parent Lease Obligations'; a curly brace above the bar groups a text list of components. A dashed yellow horizontal reference line (#F5B800) runs across both bars at about y 46.7 percent. A dark slate arrow points from the component list to the top area of the right chart and a blue-grey arrow points from the '$4.2b Parent Guaranty' line to the right chart. Right: a stacked column chart titled 'Parent Lease Obligations Reduce Over Time(3)'. Y axis: '($mm)', ticks 0 to 6,000 at 1,000 intervals with labels '-' (zero), 1,000, 2,000, 3,000, 4,000, 5,000, 6,000; no values printed on the bars. X axis: categories 6/30/2019, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026, 2027, 2028, 2029, 2030, 2031, 2032. Each column is stacked: a lower light blue-grey segment (#6E98AF) for Parent Guaranty and an upper dark slate segment (#304957) for Letters of Credit; estimated totals are about 5,100 (6/30/2019), 6,300 (2019), 6,100 (2020), 5,600 (2021), 5,200 (2022), 4,900 (2023), 4,600 (2024), 4,300 (2025), 4,000 (2026), 3,700 (2027), 3,500 (2028), 3,200 (2029), 2,900 (2030), 2,700 (2031), 2,500 (2032), all marked about. Lower segments are about 4,200 to 1,900 and upper segments about 900 to 600 (about). A dark label reads '$932mm Letters of Credit / $4.2bn Parent Guaranty' above the first column with a brace over the first column; a diagonal dark line with arrowhead runs from above column 2019 down to the right to '~52% decrease', and a second arrow continues to the 2032 column annotated '$520mm Letters of Credit / $1.9bn Parent Guaranty' with a brace under the last column. No legend.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF; flat, minimal corporate style with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Titles and body text near-black #1E1E1E; subtitle in italic dark grey #5A5A5A. Accents: teal #408F9E for the rent bars and dollar figures, blue-grey #6E98AF for parent guaranty elements, dark slate #304957 for letters of credit, yellow #F5B800 dashed reference line. Typeface: geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham or Avenir, regular weight, the main title in a larger regular weight; the chart labels appear to be ArialMT. Title text is about 5.5 percent of slide height; section headings about 3 percent; body bullets about 2.2 percent; footnote about 0.95 percent; chart labels about 1.1 percent. Thin dark rules (#3A3A3A, about 0.2 percent of height) under the section headings. Braces are thin grey-blue strokes.

- body at x 2.9, y 58.7, w 38.8, h 12 [geometric sans-serif, regular; font size 2.22% of slide height; #1E1E1E; left aligned]: Most of our locations are leased by a 100%-owned SPE that acts as the lessee and operator of that location. / The landlord typically requires some credit support of the SPE in the form of a WeWork parent guaranty and either (1) letters of credit, (2) surety bonds, or (3) cash security based on deal- and jurisdiction-specific factors and generally tapers down as the property matures. (bullet list with solid round bullets at x 3.1 percent, two bullets, line spacing about 3.0 percent)
- body at x 44.2, y 65, w 50, h 32.5 [geometric sans-serif, regular; font size 2.22% of slide height; #1E1E1E; left aligned]: For a majority of Parent Guaranties, the guaranteed amount burns off over time (though usually not to zero). / The increase in Parent Guaranties in the second half of 2019 and 2020 compared to 6/30/2019 is driven by signing additional Gate A deals. Typically, a Parent Guaranty is required on day 1 of a lease. / Landlords typically require 12-18 months of rent in the form of Letters of Credits at lease commencement. Over time, Letters of Credits gradually burn off to 6-12 months of rent. / The increase in Letters of Credit in the second half of 2019 and 2020 compared to 6/30/2019 is driven by leases that we signed in 2019 but have not yet taken possession of the property or represents the additional floors that we have signed in our existing locations, but have not yet taken possession. In this scenario, we often push not to deliver Letters of Credit at first, but the amount steps up over time as we take possession of the building or additional floors. (bullet list with solid round bullets at x 44.2 percent, four bullets, line spacing about 3.0 percent)
- footer at x 2.7, y 83.7, w 38.7, h 14 [geometric sans-serif, regular; font size 0.95% of slide height; #1E1E1E; left aligned]: Note: All numbers are as of June 30, 2019, per the Company's Lease Security Report. Parent guaranties and Letters of Credit that are associated with leases that commence in 2020 and beyond have been grouped within the 2019 vintage for illustrative purposes as we are aware of the additional obligations with each property and the majority of which are associated with existing properties where we plan to take on an additional floor and will realize a subsequent increase in parent lease obligations in the coming years. Since providing this information on October 20, 2019, the Company has revised these figures. / (1) Total Rent Commitment represents the future undiscounted fixed minimum least cost payments under operating and finance leases signed as of June 30, 2019. / (2) Guaranty for L&T is for the life of the lease for all contractual lease payments. / (3) Based on all leases commenced as of June 30, 2019 and excludes the L&T Guaranty. (small footnote text with numbered items (1) to (3) hanging-indented, line spacing about 1.5 percent)
- page number at x 96.9, y 95.8, w 2, h 2 [geometric sans-serif, regular; font size 2.2% of slide height; #8C8C8C; right aligned]: 48

49

**Layout**

Full-width title at the top left with a small italic subtitle beneath it, then two side-by-side chart panels of equal width in the upper-middle band, each with a bold section heading and a thin full-width rule above its plot. A single bullet statement sits across the lower band above a small two-line footnote block at the bottom left, with the page number at the bottom right and the word Confidential in italics at the top right corner. Left margin about 2.7% of slide width; the title and subtitle are left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Two stacked column charts. Left chart titled Parent Guaranties(1) with a y-axis labelled ($mm) running 0 to 6,000 with gridline ticks at 1,000 intervals and a x-axis of 15 categories from 6/30/2019 to YE 2032. Each column is stacked by vintage 2010 to 2019 in the legend colours (2015 light grey #BABABA, 2016 grey #9A9A9A, 2017 light blue #8EB1BA, 2018 slate grey #8A9196, 2019 orange #FDCB84, with small segments of yellow, navy and teal for 2011 to 2014 at the base). Labelled values: 6/30/2019 $4.2B (about 4,160 total), YE 2032 $1.9B (about 1,930 total); other column totals are not printed, approximately: YE 2019 about 5,040, YE 2020 about 4,960, YE 2021 about 4,710, YE 2022 about 4,380, YE 2023 about 4,060, YE 2024 about 3,720, YE 2025 about 3,400, YE 2026 about 3,180, YE 2027 about 2,950, YE 2028 about 2,780, YE 2029 about 2,530, YE 2030 about 2,310, YE 2031 about 2,160. A black thin diagonal arrow runs from above the first column down to the last column with the annotation ~53% decrease in the centre. Right chart titled Letters of Credit(1), same colour stacking, no y-axis labels, 15 categories from 6/30/2019 to YE 2032; labelled values $932 at 6/30/2019 and $520 at YE 2032; others about, from left to right roughly 1,100, 1,080, 1,070, 1,030, 1,000, 980, 960, 940, 900, 880, 850, 820, 780, 720 (estimates). A thin black arrow runs from above the first column to the last with the annotation ~44% decrease. No highlighted points; the legend appears only above the left chart as a single horizontal row of ten small square markers.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decoration. Title in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham or Avenir-like) regular weight, charcoal #2A2D34. Subtitle in italic sans-serif grey #6B6B6B. Section headings in medium-weight geometric sans #2A2D34 with superscript (1). Chart labels, legend and footnotes in Arial regular #1E1E1E. Bullet statement in geometric sans regular #1E1E1E. Chart series in pale palette: orange #FDCB84, slate #8A9196, light blue #8EB1BA, greys #9A9A9A and #BABABA, with thin dark #1E1E1E arrows and rules. Title text about 6 percent of slide height, body about 2.6 percent, footnotes about 1.2 percent. Rules are 0.1 percent of height, dark grey #1E1E1E. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.
